# Corpus census — 20260829-174815

Read-only. Nothing renamed, uploaded, inserted or deleted.

Regenerated after the 29 August imports. Supersedes `census-20260829-130922.md`,
which described the corpus at 3,512 PDFs before that day's work.

## Bucket precedence

Each file is assigned to the FIRST matching bucket. Buckets are mutually exclusive.

1 corrupt/unreadable PDF
2 not an exam paper at all
3 Z-code excluded
4 SAM/EAM specimen excluded
5 orphan/non-paper component
6 unsupported/unparseable filename
7 exact duplicate (byte-identical)
8 canonical collision (same key, different bytes)
9 unresolved session
10 already represented in past_papers
11 ready to import
12 other blocker

### Correction — 2026-08-29

**Bucket 12's 20 files are IMPORTED, not blocked.** The line above describes them
as held back by `MAX_YEAR = 2025`. That is how this census SAW them, and it is
not their real status.

Those 20 files are the January 2026 IAL papers — WCH11/12/14/15 (QU+MS each) and
WPH11/12/14/15 (QU+MS+ER each) — and they were committed earlier the same day as
**8 papers**, `unit-1/2/4/5-january-2026` under IAL Chemistry and IAL Physics,
confirmed in the database with `year = 2026`.

The census reads them as blocked because its fifteen dry runs were issued
WITHOUT `--include-year=2026`. The importer checks the year before it checks
whether a slug already exists, so those files were rejected on year and never
reached the already-exists test. They fell into bucket 12 instead of bucket 10.

This is a property of how the census was generated, not of the corpus. Had the
dry runs carried `--include-year=2026`, bucket 10 would read 2,295 and bucket 12
would read 0.

**The total is unaffected: 3,543 either way.** Every file still sits in exactly
one bucket and the arithmetic still closes. The census has NOT been recomputed —
the tally above is left exactly as it ran, with this note as the correction.


## What moved

Three buckets emptied, and that is the whole story of the day:

- **5 orphan — 1 to 0.** `9BN0_03_0623_ER.pdf` sat alone all morning. Its question
  paper and mark scheme were recovered from Pearson and it is now a whole paper.
- **8 canonical collision — 12 to 0.** The four 4CH1 January 2022 entries were all
  named `1121`, colliding with the genuine November 2021 papers. Renamed to `0122`
  on cover evidence; both sittings now import as separate papers.
- **11 ready to import — 3 to 0.** Everything importable has been imported.

Bucket 10 rises 2,228 to 2,275 (+47), which is the 31 Pearson files plus the
components of papers that became importable once their gaps were filled.

Buckets 7 and 12 are unchanged: 978 byte-identical duplicates, and the 20
January 2026 IAL files held back by `MAX_YEAR = 2025`.

## Two properties that still hold

**Bucket 6 is empty.** All 270 files that fail the filename grammar are Z-code (30)
or specimen material (240). No unparseable residue remains.

**Bucket 2 is empty by filename evidence only.** No name matches
spec/grade-boundary/syllabus/notice/timetable, and no PDF was opened to inspect
its content. It means "nothing declares itself non-exam in its name", not
"nothing is non-exam".
